<h6 style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', serif; font-size: 12pt; font-style: italic; color: blue; text-align: center; margin-bottom: 5px; margin-top: 15px;">Table 3. Business Rule</h6>
<table class="c196"><tr class="c134"><td class="c35 c421" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c7"><span class="c11">ID</span></p></td><td class="c39 c421" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c7"><span class="c11">Rule Definition</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c290"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-1</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Universal Form Data Validation:</span><span class="c2"> All submitted form fields must pass strict data type, format, length, and value constraints (e.g., report descriptions must be at least 20 characters, and confidence thresholds must be decimals between 0.01 and 1.0) before the system processes the submission.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-2</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Unique Reasons:</span><span class="c2"> The user cannot select the same report reason more than once in a single submission.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-3</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Reason Selection:</span><span class="c2"> A valid violation reason (Spam, Offensive, Fake, Other) must be selected before submission.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-4</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Duplicate Report Prevention:</span><span class="c2"> A user cannot report the same review/course with the same reason if their previous report is still pending.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-5</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Stripe Link Limits:</span><span class="c2"> Instructors without an active Stripe Connect profile may publish up to 2 courses with a maximum of 30 minutes of video content.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-6</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Free Course Video Ceiling:</span><span class="c2"> Courses priced at 0 are capped at 60 minutes of video content. Paid courses require an active Stripe Connect configuration.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-7</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Content Re-activation:</span><span class="c2"> On resubmission, the system automatically resets any previously rejected lesson and material statuses back to active for admin review.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-8</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Locked Out Instructor Rights:</span><span class="c2"> Instructors with an active lockout status are prohibited from submitting courses for review or changing course status.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-9</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Default View Of Course Reports:</span><span class="c2"> The Report Center always opens with the Course Reports tab and the "Pending" status filter selected by default.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-10</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Access Control Of Report Center:</span><span class="c2"> Only authenticated Staff and Admin actors are permitted to access the Report Center.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-11</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Status Badges:</span><span class="c2"> Report statuses (Pending, Resolved, Rejected, Escalated) are visually distinguished using specific status badges.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-12</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Action Availability:</span><span class="c2"> The "Moderate" action button is available only for reports in Pending, Under Review, or Escalated status. All other statuses display an "Info" button instead.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-13</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Staff Escalation Limitation:</span><span class="c2"> Staff actors cannot moderate Escalated reports. Only Admins may moderate reports in the Escalated status.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-14</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Default View Of Course Reviews Subtab:</span><span class="c2"> When the Review Reports tab is accessed, the system defaults to the "Course Reviews" subtab with the "Pending" status filter applied.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c290"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-15</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Adaptive Moderation/Detail Popup UI:</span><span class="c2"> The moderation and detail popups dynamically adjust titles, fields, labels, and action terminologies (e.g., "Flag Course" vs "Remove content") depending on whether a course or a review is being viewed/moderated.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-16</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Resolution Visibility:</span><span class="c2"> Resolution details (Moderator, Resolved At, Resolution Note) are visible only for reports with a resolved or rejected status.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-17</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Independent Status Filters:</span><span class="c2"> The status filter in the Course Reports tab maintains its own independent state from the status filter in the Review Reports tab.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-18</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Subtab Filter Retention:</span><span class="c2"> When switching between the "Course Reviews" and "Lesson Reviews" subtabs, the currently active status filter is retained and applied to the newly selected subtype.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-19</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Universal Empty State Display:</span><span class="c2"> Whenever a list retrieval operation yields no records (either due to an empty database or active filter criteria), the system displays a user-friendly empty state message instead of a blank table or list container.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-20</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Escalation Restriction:</span><span class="c2"> Staff may select the "Escalate to Senior Admin" option. Admins do not see this option, as they are the highest escalation tier.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-21</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Action Limitations:</span><span class="c2"> The "Flag Course / Remove content" checkbox is enabled exclusively when the Resolution Status is set to "Accept Report". Changing the status automatically clears and disables the checkbox.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-22</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Course Strike System:</span><span class="c2"> When a course report is accepted with the "Flag Course" toggle checked: Strike 1 or 2 sends a warning to the instructor; Strike 3 permanently archives the course and applies a 30-day severe instructor lockout.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c290"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-23</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Review Penalty System:</span><span class="c2"> When a review report is accepted with "Remove content" checked: Strike 1 sends a warning; Strike 2 applies a 7-day moderate review lockout; Strike 3 applies a 30-day severe full account ban and terminates active sessions.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-24</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Implicit Refresh:</span><span class="c2"> A successful moderation action automatically triggers a silent refresh of the current report list and overall statistics.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c290"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-25</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Admin Authorization for AI Services:</span><span class="c2"> Only authenticated Admin actors are authorized to access and perform actions within the AI Service and Model Management module (including adding/updating models, toggling status, viewing details/list, configuring thresholds, and viewing activity logs).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c290"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-26</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">List Sorting and Ordering:</span><span class="c2"> Administrative lists (such as the AI model list and activity logs) are sorted in descending order of their latest timestamp (last modified date for models, creation date for logs), ensuring that the most recent entries are displayed first.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-27</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Pre-populated Data:</span><span class="c2"> Model details displayed in the popup are sourced from data embedded in the page during the initial render; no additional server request is made on click.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-28</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Universal List Pagination:</span><span class="c2"> All list retrieval operations displaying tabular or grid data must display a limited number of entries per page with pagination controls.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-29</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Default Subtab Of Activity Log:</span><span class="c2"> The course moderation category is displayed by default when the logs tab is opened.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-30</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Status Default:</span><span class="c2"> The model status defaults to active upon creation.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-31</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Status Toggle:</span><span class="c2"> Toggling an active model sets its status to inactive; toggling an inactive model restores it to active.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-32</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Read Status Sync:</span><span class="c2"> Viewing a notification's details modal automatically marks it as read in the database and updates the UI state.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-33</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Ownership Constraint:</span><span class="c2"> A user or instructor can only mark their own received notifications as read.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-34</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Confirmation Prompt:</span><span class="c2"> Deleting a notification requires confirmation via a dialog to prevent accidental deletion.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-35</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Cart in list:</span><span class="c2"> Cart item prices must reflect the current database prices including any active platform discounts.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-36</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Default View:</span><span class="c2"> By default, all active conversations are retrieved and displayed in "Recently Active" sorting order (newest last message at the top).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-37</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Online Status Indicator:</span><span class="c2"> A green dot is displayed on the partner's avatar if the partner is currently connected to the chat hub.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-38</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Sender Peer Constraint:</span><span class="c2"> Staff and Admin users cannot send notifications to themselves. Staff members are blocked from sending notifications to other Staff or Admin users.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-39</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Recipient Selection Required:</span><span class="c2"> If the target group is "Specific User", at least one valid email tag must be specified</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c290"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-40</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Auto Read State Synchronization:</span><span class="c2"> If a Staff user views a notification specifically addressed to them that is unread, the system must trigger a status transition to "Read". Admins or other Staff users viewing the notification do not trigger this transition.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-41</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Verified Email Constraint:</span><span class="c2"> Only Actors with a verified email address are allowed to add items to their cart.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-42</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Purchase and Ownership Restrictions:</span><span class="c2"> An Actor cannot add their own courses (where they are the instructor) or already</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-43</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Item Uniqueness:</span><span class="c2"> A course can only be added to an Actor's cart once.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-44</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Price Recalculation:</span><span class="c2"> Deleting an item must immediately trigger a recalculation of the cart's subtotal, discount, and grand total, and update any applied coupons.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-45</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Personal Cleared History:</span><span class="c2"> Clearing chat history only sets the cleared timestamp for the active participant. The conversation history remains intact and visible to other participants.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-46</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Real-Time Synchronization:</span><span class="c2"> Typing state, online status, and message transmission are updated instantly via SignalR callbacks.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-47</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Role promotion:</span><span class="c2"> Approving an application triggers a role promotion for the target user from "User" to "Instructor".</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-48</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Rejection Reason Required:</span><span class="c2"> A non-empty text justification is mandatory before an instructor application can be rejected.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-49</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Public Visibility:</span><span class="c2"> The course list displays only courses that are currently published. Draft, pending, rejected, archived, or locked courses are excluded from the public list.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-50</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Wishlist Sync:</span><span class="c2"> Wishlist heart icons reflect the current wishlist status of the logged-in User in real-time, displaying a filled red heart for wishlisted courses and an unfilled icon otherwise.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-51</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Cart Status Integration:</span><span class="c2"> If a logged-in User has already purchased/enrolled in a course, the "Add to cart" button is replaced by a checkmark icon to prevent double purchases.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-52</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Debounced Live Search:</span><span class="c2"> Real-time keyword filtering is debounced by 350ms to minimize API calls during continuous typing.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-53</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">View Mode Retention:</span><span class="c2"> User layout preference (Grid vs. List view) is stored in the browser's local storage and persists across sessions.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-54</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">State Persistence:</span><span class="c2"> Active filter parameters are stored in the browser's local storage and are automatically applied when the user returns to the page without URL parameters.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c290"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-55</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Curriculum Locking:</span><span class="c2"> Only the first material of the first lesson of the first section is available for preview to non-enrolled actors. All subsequent materials are visually locked (showing a lock icon) and cannot be accessed from the details page.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c290"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-56</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Unpublished Course Access Restrictions:</span><span class="c2"> Courses with statuses other than "published" (e.g., draft, pending, archived) are strictly hidden from the public and can only be viewed by the original instructor, system administrators, or students who were previously enrolled.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-57</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Review Visibility:</span><span class="c2"> Course reviews and rating statistics are publicly visible to all actors.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-58</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Wishlist State:</span><span class="c2"> The wishlist toggle option is only displayed to Users. Instructors viewing their own course and system administrators cannot add courses to their wishlist.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-59</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Course Reporting:</span><span class="c2"> The option to report a course is only visible to enrolled students. It is hidden from the course owner, administrators, and for courses that are pending or already permanently archived with 3 or more flags.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-60</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Review Eligibility:</span><span class="c2"> The ability to write and submit a course review is strictly limited to authenticated Users who are enrolled in the course and have completed 100% of the lessons.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-61</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Debounced Query Execution:</span><span class="c2"> Real-time keyword filtering is debounced by 350ms to minimize API calls during continuous typing.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-62</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Keyword Match Scope:</span><span class="c2"> Search query checks against course titles, instructor names, short descriptions, and categories.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-63</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Published Status Only:</span><span class="c2"> Only courses with "published" status are returned in the search results.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-64</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Filter Combinability:</span><span class="c2"> Multiple filters (category, price, rating, keyword) can be applied simultaneously, narrowing the results conjunctively (AND operation).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-65</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Rating Filter Bounds:</span><span class="c2"> Rating filters select courses with average rating greater than or equal to the selected threshold (e.g., &gt;= 4.5, &gt;= 4.0, &gt;= 3.5).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-66</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Price Filter Bounds:</span><span class="c2"> Price filter options classify courses as "Free" (Price == 0) or "Paid" (Price &gt; 0).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-67</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Newest Ordering:</span><span class="c2"> Courses are sorted by their creation date in descending order.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-68</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Highest Rated Ordering:</span><span class="c2"> Courses are sorted by their average rating in descending order.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-69</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Price Low-to-High Ordering:</span><span class="c2"> Courses are sorted by their price in ascending order.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-70</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Price High-to-Low Ordering:</span><span class="c2"> Courses are sorted by their price in descending order.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-71</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Sort Order Persistence:</span><span class="c2"> The selected sorting parameter is saved in the browser's local storage and persists across page reloads.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-72</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Course Visibility:</span><span class="c2"> Only courses with status "Published" are displayed on the public profile. Draft, pending, rejected, or archived courses are strictly excluded.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-73</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Profile Access:</span><span class="c2"> A public profile is only accessible if the instructor's account application status is "Approved".</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-74</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Social Links:</span><span class="c2"> Social media links ("LinkedIn", "YouTube", "Facebook") are only rendered as clickable buttons/links if the instructor has provided the respective URLs.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-75</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Anonymous Access:</span><span class="c2"> Anyone (including Guests) can view public course reviews and statistics.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c290"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-76</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Review Masking (Removed Reviews):</span><span class="c2"> If a review has been removed by the moderator (status "violating"), the content is replaced with a warning notification: "This review was removed by a moderator for violating community standards." and the rating stars are set to 0. If a review has been self-deleted by the user, it is omitted from the course details list.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-77</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Paging Size:</span><span class="c2"> The reviews list on the details page is paginated with a limit of 5 reviews per page.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-78</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">General vs Lesson Badges:</span><span class="c2"> General course-level reviews are flagged with a "General review" badge, while lesson-level reviews (originally submitted in the player) are flagged with the corresponding lesson title.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-79</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Progress Calculation:</span><span class="c2"> Course progress percentage is calculated as (Completed Materials / Total Materials) * 100, rounded to 1 decimal place.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-80</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Video Completion Trigger:</span><span class="c2"> A video material is marked as completed only when the video playback reaches the end ("onended" event).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-81</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Document/Text Completion Trigger:</span><span class="c2"> A text document (".txt") is marked as completed immediately upon viewing. Other downloadable resources are marked as completed when the User clicks the download button.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-82</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Course Completion State:</span><span class="c2"> When all learning materials in a course are marked as completed, the system automatically sets the enrollment status IsCompleted = true and updates the completion date in the database.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-83</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Instructor Bypass:</span><span class="c2"> Course owners/instructors can access the Course Player ("Learn" page) for preview, and their progress is treated as 100% completed virtual progress without writing completions to the database.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-84</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Dynamic Button Update:</span><span class="c2"> For free courses, when an un-enrolled User clicks "Enroll Now", the page successfully processes the enrollment and dynamically updates the button to "Go to learning" without a full page reload.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-85</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Unpublished Course Warning:</span><span class="c2"> If the course is not in the published state, a warning banner is displayed at the top of the course player, but access remains permitted for owners and managers.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-86</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Protected Media:</span><span class="c2"> Video lectures are served with playback controls only; direct download option is disabled (utilizing "controlsList" attribute set to "nodownload").</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-87</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Completed Badge Display:</span><span class="c2"> A completed course card displays a distinct completed overlay badge with the text "Completed" and a "check_circle" icon.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-88</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Progress Bar Color Coding:</span><span class="c2"> The progress bar is colored emerald green if the course is 100% completed, and the primary color (teal) otherwise.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-89</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Dynamic Actions based on Completion:</span><span class="c2"> The action button text is set to class="c2">BR-91</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Double Purchase Prevention:</span><span class="c2"> The system blocks purchase/checkout of class="c2">BR-93</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Completion Check for Course Review:</span><span class="c2"> Non-owner students must have 100% class="c2">BR-94</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Minimal Progress for Lesson Review:</span><span class="c2"> Non-owner students must have class="c2">BR-95</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Rating Restrictions:</span><span class="c2"> Star ratings must be between 1 and 5 stars class="c6"><span class="c11">Account Restrictions (Lockout):</span><span class="c2"> Users with active comment/review restrictions are blocked from submitting new reviews. The system checks this validation at submission time.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-97</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Instructor Notification:</span><span class="c2"> Upon successful submission, a notification is sent to the course instructor with a link pointing directly to the review card.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-98</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Self-Ownership Only:</span><span class="c2"> An actor can only delete reviews they have authored.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-99</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Moderation Block:</span><span class="c2"> If a review has an active pending moderation report, deletion is blocked until the moderation report is resolved.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-100</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Soft Delete:</span><span class="c2"> Deletion is implemented as a soft-delete (setting `IsRemoved = true` and updating status to "removed" in the database). It does not permanently delete the record.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-101</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Rating Statistics Recalculation:</span><span class="c2"> Upon successful deletion, the overall rating average and counts are dynamically recalculated to exclude the deleted review.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-102</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Title Length Constraints:</span><span class="c2"> The course title must be between 5 and 100 characters in length.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-103</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Text Constraints:</span><span class="c2"> What You Will Learn and Requirements descriptions must each be at least 20 characters in length (excluding HTML tags).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-104</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Stripe Payout Connectivity:</span><span class="c2"> Instructors must connect and activate their Stripe accounts to set premium prices. If Stripe is disconnected or inactive, the course price is locked to 0.00 (Free).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-105</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Voucher Validation:</span><span class="c2"> Platform sponsored discount vouchers cannot be applied to free courses.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-106</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Draft Initial State:</span><span class="c2"> All newly created courses are saved with a status of "Draft" and must go through moderation before being published.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-107</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Status Transition Restrictions:</span><span class="c2"> An instructor can only set a course to status 'pending' (moderation), 'archived' (hidden), or 'published' (only when unarchiving from status 'archived').</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-108</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Permanently Discontinued Block:</span><span class="c2"> A course that is 'archived' and has 3 or more flags is considered permanently discontinued due to policy violations and cannot have its status changed or be edited.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-109</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Active Enrollment Preservation:</span><span class="c2"> Archiving a course does not delete it or impact active student enrollments; active students enrolled in the course continue to have full access to its contents on their dashboards.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-110</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Auto-Purge Policy:</span><span class="c2"> Learning materials placed in the trash bin are automatically and permanently deleted after 30 days by a background cleanup service.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-111</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Cloud Storage Virtual Directory:</span><span class="c2"> Moving a file to the trash bin renames the file prefix on Cloudinary to `trash/` and updates its status to "removed" in the database, hiding it from the public display while retaining the database record.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-112</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Video Swap Constraint:</span><span class="c2"> Restoring a video material to a lesson that already contains an active video will automatically move the current active video to the trash bin, replacing it with the restored video.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-113</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Discontinued &amp; Pending Locking:</span><span class="c2"> Learning materials cannot be restored or permanently deleted if the parent course is pending review or is permanently discontinued due to policy violations.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-114</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Unpublish Trigger:</span><span class="c2"> Restoring a learning material to a published course will automatically update the course status back to "Draft" to force re-moderation of the updated curriculum.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-115</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Draft Demotion Rule:</span><span class="c2"> If the parent course is currently "Published", restoring any curriculum material will automatically update the course status to "Draft" and clear the moderation feedback, requiring the instructor to re-submit it for review.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-116</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Rights &amp; Mod Lockouts:</span><span class="c2"> Restoration is locked if the instructor is under active suspension/lockout, or if the parent course is pending review, or if the parent course is permanently discontinued due to 3 or more flags.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-117</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Cloud Storage Synchronization:</span><span class="c2"> Restoring a file dynamically renames it in Cloudinary to remove the `trash/` prefix, syncing the physical path with the database status.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-118</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Irreversible Deletion:</span><span class="c2"> Permanent deletion removes both the database record and the asset hosted on cloud storage (Cloudinary) and cannot be recovered or restored.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-119</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Cloud Storage Prefix:</span><span class="c2"> The file is deleted from the virtual trash path (prefix `trash/`) in cloud storage using its `CloudPublicId`.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-120</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Exclusion of Drafts:</span><span class="c2"> Courses with status "Draft" are never retrieved or displayed in the moderation queues.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c628"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-121</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Urgency Level Calculation:</span><span class="c2"> For pending courses, the urgency color is calculated based on creation date:</span></p><ul class="c0 lst-kix_m4ehr1cck2sk-0 start"><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Red / "High" urgency: pending &gt; 72 hours.</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Amber / "Medium" urgency: pending &gt; 24 hours.</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Slate / "Normal" urgency: pending &lt;= 24 hours.</span></li></ul></td></tr><tr class="c733"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-122</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Threat Levels:</span><span class="c2"> Threat levels correspond to AI check feedback:</span></p><ul class="c0 lst-kix_ba16uow5o4od-0 start"><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Critical Threat (FlaggedOrRejected): course hits critical policy violations.</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">High Threat (ManualAudit): course needs manual audit.</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Clean (Approved): course is approved by automated check.</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Pending / None: default.</span></li></ul></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-123</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Infraction Flags:</span><span class="c2"> A course tracks up to 3 flags. If flag count reaches 3, status is changed to "Archived" and course status maps to "Permanently Locked".</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c563"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-124</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Search Criteria:</span><span class="c2"> Search keywords are matched (case-insensitively, substring match) against:</span></p><ul class="c0 lst-kix_xe3cpt2zgny8-0 start"><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Course Title</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Instructor Name (Full Name)</span></li></ul></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-125</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Preservation of Filters:</span><span class="c2"> Searching preserves all other active filters (Category, Status, Sort Option).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-126</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Debounce Time:</span><span class="c2"> To prevent excessive server requests, the search execution is debounced by 300 milliseconds from the last keystroke.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c733"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-127</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c10">Sorting Options:</span></p><ul class="c0 lst-kix_8rpqrhrj113p-0 start"><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">"Threat Level (High to Low)" ("threat_desc"): Sorts descending by threat level (Critical Threat &gt; High Threat &gt; Clean &gt; None), then ascending by creation date. This is the default sort.</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">"Threat Level (Low to High)" ("threat_asc"): Sorts ascending by threat level, then ascending by creation date.</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">"Oldest (Priority)" ("oldest"): Sorts ascending by creation/submission date (oldest first).</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">"Newest" ("newest"): Sorts descending by creation/submission date (newest first).</span></li></ul></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-128</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Category Filter:</span><span class="c2"> Matches the Category ID or Name against the course's category. If "All Categories" is selected, no category filtering is applied.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-129</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Preservation of Other Criteria:</span><span class="c2"> Filtering preserves active search keywords and sorting options.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-130</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Re-approval Rule:</span><span class="c2"> If the course status is rejected, archived, or permanently locked, the action button is labeled "Re-approve" but follows the same approval logic.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-131</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Notification Content:</span><span class="c2"> The approval notification sent to the instructor includes the optional feedback entered by the actor.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-132</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Cache Eviction:</span><span class="c2"> The Redis cache for the course details must be evicted immediately upon approval to ensure users see the updated content.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c628"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-133</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c10">Violation Element Tagging:</span></p><ul class="c0 lst-kix_anyvv6d9xfjc-0 start"><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Course-level targets correspond to: title ("course.title"), description ("course.description"), thumbnail ("course.thumbnail"), what you will learn ("course.what_you_will_learn"), requirements ("course.requirements").</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Lesson-level targets correspond to "lesson.title".</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c2">Material-level targets correspond to "file".</span></li></ul></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-134</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Guest Login Prompt:</span><span class="c2"> System prompts Guest users to log in when clicking on interactive elements on the homepage.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-135</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Combined Feedback:</span><span class="c2"> The course's "ModerationFeedback" is updated by combining all tagged element reasons (e.g. "[@Title] Reason" or "[Lesson: Title] Reason" separated by newlines).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-136</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Notification Link:</span><span class="c2"> The rejection notification sent to the instructor contains a link redirecting to the course editor "/InstructorCourse/Editor?id={courseId}".</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c134"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-137</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Flag Count Limit:</span><span class="c2"> A course can have a maximum of 3 flags. Flag count cannot exceed 3.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c628"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-138</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c10">Strike Warnings:</span></p><ul class="c0 lst-kix_9514qdtosl46-0 start"><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c11">Strike 1 (1st Flag):</span><span class="c2"> Sends notification "Course Violation Reminder (1st Time)". The course is temporarily hidden. Instructor is reminded to review and comply.</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c11">Strike 2 (2nd Flag):</span><span class="c2"> Sends notification "Severe Violation Warning (2nd Time)". Course remains hidden. Warning that 3rd flag leads to permanent lock.</span></li><li class="c97 c60 li-bullet-0"><span class="c11">Strike 3 (3rd Flag):</span><span class="c2"> Sends notice "Permanent Course Discontinuation Notice (3rd Time)". Status changes to "archived" (Permanently Locked). Instructor is blocked from editing, and no new enrollments are allowed.</span></li></ul></td></tr><tr class="c648"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-139</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Debounce Optimization:</span><span class="c33"> Auto-saving uses a 1.5-second debounce delay to prevent database spamming during rapid typing.</span><span class="c10"> </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c628"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-140</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Draft Reversion:</span><span class="c33"> To ensure content integrity, any edits made to an already "published" course will automatically degrade its status back to "draft", requiring a new moderation review submission and clearing previous moderation feedback.</span><span class="c10"> </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-141</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Content Integrity Hashing:</span><span class="c33"> Textual details and the thumbnail file are hashed using MD5 and stored as a unique fingerprint to prevent duplication and support anti-plagiarism tracking.</span><span class="c10"> </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-142</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Lesson Limit for Unlinked Stripe Accounts:</span><span class="c2"> Instructors who have not linked an active Stripe Connect profile are only allowed to create up to 5 lessons per course.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-143</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Lesson Title Uniqueness: </span><span class="c2">Lesson titles must be unique within a course (case-insensitive).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-144</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Pending Review Modification Lock:</span><span class="c2"> Learning materials and lessons cannot be added, edited, or removed if the course is currently in 'Pending' review status.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-145</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Syllabus Change Reversion: </span><span class="c2">Adding or removing lessons and materials, or editing their content, in a 'Published' course automatically changes the course status back to 'Draft' and clears any existing moderation feedback.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-146</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Lesson Soft Deletion:</span><span class="c2"> Deleting a lesson soft-deletes the lesson record and all its associated materials (IsRemoved = true) in the database, and moves physical cloud files to the trash.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-147</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Supplementary Document Limits:</span><span class="c2"> Instructors who have not linked an active Stripe Connect profile are only allowed to attach up to 2 supplementary documents per lesson.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-148</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Material Soft Deletion:</span><span class="c2"> Removing a material does not permanently delete the file; the physical file is moved to the Cloud Storage Service's trash folder and its database status is updated to 'Removed'.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c651"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-149</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Active Student Deletion Restriction:</span><span class="c2"> Courses with active student enrollments cannot be deleted and must be archived instead.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-150</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Course Soft Deletion: </span><span class="c2">Deleting a course soft-deletes both the course and all its associated lessons and materials (IsRemoved = true) in the database, rather than permanently destroying the records.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-151</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Instructor Dashboard Access Restriction: </span><span class="c33">Only fully approved instructors are allowed to access the Instructor Dashboard; other states trigger redirects to application status or creation workflows.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-152</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Course Status Badging: </span><span class="c2">The system visually distinguishes courses on the instructor dashboard using specific badges based on their current status (e.g., Published, Pending Review, Draft, Rejected, Archived, Removed, Permanently Locked).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-153</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Contextual Dashboard Actions: </span><span class="c2">Course management actions on the instructor dashboard are determined dynamically based on course status; permanently locked or removed courses have severely restricted action sets.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c651"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-154</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Instructor Data Isolation: Instructors </span><span class="c33">can only view statistics, revenue, and courses associated with their own account.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-155</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Enrollment Pre-check: </span><span class="c2">The system  checks recipient enrollment as the user types in the email field (debounced). The "Proceed to Payment" button is disabled until the check confirms the recipient is not enrolled.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-156</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Gift Price Restrictions: </span><span class="c2">Gift purchases are always made at the course's original price. Promotional coupons and discount codes are not applicable to gift purchases.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-157</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Claim and Refund Mutual Exclusion: </span><span class="c2">Once a gift is successfully claimed by the recipient, any pending refund request for that gift transaction is automatically rejected.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-158</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">No Cart Modification:</span><span class="c2"> Gifting is treated as a direct purchase and does not clear or modify the items in the user's active shopping cart.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-159</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">No Double Enrollment: </span><span class="c2">A user cannot claim a gift course if they already possess an active enrollment in that course</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-160</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Unclaimed Refund Restriction:</span><span class="c2"> Gifts can only be claimed if they have not been refunded. A refund reverses the redemption token validity.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-161</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">14-Day Escrow Policy: </span><span class="c2">Refund requests are only permitted for successful transactions (Success status) within a 14-day escrow window from the purchase date. Once this window expires, the refund request button is disabled/hidden.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-162</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Escrow Hold: </span><span class="c2">Instructor accounts are not credited with the course revenue until the 14-day refund window expires, ensuring funds are available for refund payouts.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-163</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11"> Pagination Limit: </span><span class="c2">Transaction history results are paginated with a default of 10 items per page</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-164</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Enhanced Search Component</span><span class="c2"> : The search input field must retain the entered keyword after the page reloads so the user knows which filter is currently active</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-165</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Course Title Matching:</span><span class="c2">The search is performed as a case-insensitive substring match on the Course Title</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-166</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">Auto-Rejection - Account Flag:</span><span class="c33"> A refund request is automatically rejected if the user account currently has 3 or more warning flags (warning count &gt;= 3).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-167</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c10">Auto-Rejection - Refund Frequency</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c2">A refund request is automatically rejected if the user has requested 3 or more refunds within the last 14 days.</span></p><p class="c6 c23"><span class="c10"></span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-168</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c10">Auto-Rejection - Repeated Course Refund</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c2">A refund request is automatically rejected if the user has previously requested and received a successful refund for the exact same course.</span></p><p class="c6 c23"><span class="c10"></span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-169</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c10">Auto-Rejection - Progress for Short Courses (&lt; 4h)</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c2">For courses with a total duration of less than 4 hours, a refund request is automatically rejected if the student's learning progress exceeds 15%.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-170</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">BR-05: </span><span class="c2">Auto-Rejection - Progress for Long Courses (&gt;= 4h)</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c2">For courses with a total duration of 4 hours or more, a refund request is automatically rejected if the student's completed video watch time exceeds 1 hour.</span></p><p class="c6 c23"><span class="c10"></span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-171</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c10"> Abstract Reason Confidentiality</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c2">All auto-rejection notifications must strictly display abstract descriptions for the reject reason. It is forbidden to expose specific numeric criteria (such as exact percentage values, exact watch hours, number of flags, or refund counts) to the user. The abstract descriptions displayed are:</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c2">- Account flags limit: "your account having multiple flags"</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c2">- Refund frequency limit: "having requested too many refunds within the refund period"</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c2">- Repeated refund: "previous refund history for this course"</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c2">- Progress limit for short course: "learning progress exceeding the limit for short courses"</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c33">- Watch time limit for long course: "video watch time exceeding the limit allowed for long courses"</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-172</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c10">Refund Eligibility Window</span></p><p class="c6"><span class="c2">A refund request must be submitted within 14 days (336 hours) from the original transaction timestamp.</span></p><p class="c6 c23"><span class="c10"></span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-173</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c12"><span class="c11">Negative Refund Balance</span><span class="c33"> :Display If the transaction status is marked as refunded, must display the Gross Amount and Total as negative values to represent the credited refund.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-174</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c12"><span class="c11"> Strict Application Idempotency: </span><span class="c2"> A learner account can only have one active application record. If a previous request was rejected, any subsequent submission will overwrite the old record inline to keep the database clean and prevent duplicates.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-175</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c10">File Attachment Restrictions</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c2">  - The user can upload a maximum of 3 document/certificate files.</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c2">  - Uploaded credential documents are strictly restricted to `.pdf`, `.jpg`, `.jpeg`, and `.png` extensions.</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c33">  - The maximum file upload size is 5MB per file to protect server resources.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-176</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c10">Email Verification Requirement</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c33">  Learners must have their email verified before they are permitted to submit or access the instructor application form.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-177</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11">Instructor Role Activation :</span><span class="c2">The user's role is upgraded to "instructor" in cookies and database sessions only after Stripe Connect onboarding is verified as "Active"</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-178</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11"> Direct Edit Lockout :</span><span class="c2">Once the bank account is successfully linked and status is "Active", the system hides the "Setup Payout" button. Instructors are locked out from editing bank details directly on the platform and must click the "Access Stripe Dashboard" button to edit directly on Stripe's secure portal.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-179</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11">Dynamic Balances Calculation:</span><span class="c2">All balance cards must dynamically recalculate based on the selected month and year filter.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-180</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11"> Default Currency Display :</span><span class="c2">All balances, amounts, and chart labels default to USD (prefixed with the $ symbol).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-181</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c10">Financial Card Definitions &amp; Formulas</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c10">The financial metrics are defined and calculated as follows:</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11">- </span><span class="c2">Gross Revenue: Total sales revenue generated from course purchases.</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c2">- Total Refunded: Total deductions from learner refund requests (prefixed with a red minus - symbol).</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c2">- Net Earnings: Actual revenue remaining after subtracting refunded amounts (Net Earnings = Gross Revenue - Total Refunded).</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c2">- Pending Transfer: Funds currently locked in the 14-day escrow protection window or in transit.</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c2">- Stripe Wallet: Funds successfully moved to the instructor's Connected Stripe Wallet (Ready for Bank Transfer).</span></p><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c2">- Withdrawn: Total funds successfully transferred to the instructor's bank account.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-182</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11">Negative Payout Display for Refunded Status: </span><span class="c2">If a transaction status is "refunded", the system must display as a negative value prefixed with a minus `-` sign in red (e.g., `- $15.29`).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-183</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11">Transaction Access Control :</span><span class="c2">Instructors can only view details of transactions related to courses they own. Attempts to access other instructors' transaction details will be blocked and redirected.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-184</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11"> Expected Payout Date Calculation :</span><span class="c2">The expected payout date (`PayoutDate` field in the database) is automatically calculated at the time of purchase as the configured payout day of the next month (e.g., the 15th of the next month) relative to the transaction date. This reflects the platform's monthly billing cycle and the 14-day escrow window.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c632"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-185</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11">Sequential Numbering (No.)</span><span class="c2">The first column in the payout history table must display sequential numbers (No., e.g., `#1`, `#2`, `#3`) instead of the escrow clearance date, avoiding confusion for the use</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-186</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11">Preservation of Filters: </span><span class="c2">Selecting a sorting option and submitting the query must preserve all other active search keywords, status filters, and calendar month/year period selections.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-187</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c159 c60"><span class="c11"> Default Sort Order:</span><span class="c2">If no sorting option is explicitly selected, the list defaults to "Date: Newest" order.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-188</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c10"> Default Period:</span></p><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c33"> If no month and year are specified, the system defaults to the current UTC calendar month and year.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-189</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c10">Pagination Reset: </span></p><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c33">Modifying any filter criteria and applying it resets the transaction pagination back to page 1.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-190</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c11">  Default Sorting</span><span class="c33">: The payout list is sorted by date newest to oldest by default if no sorting option is explicitly selected.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-191</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c11">Locked Currency and Split Rates :</span><span class="c2">All split rates (Transfer Rate) and conversion rates are locked at the time of purchase to guarantee stable revenues and prevent exchange rate fluctuations</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-192</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c11">Stripe Fee Calculation :</span><span class="c2">Stripe processing fee is calculated exactly as GrossAmount * 0.029 + 0.30 (capped at the gross amount to avoid negative ledger balances).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-193</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c10">Split Summary Ledger Formulas</span></p><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c2">The Split Summary Card calculates and displays values according to the following formulas:</span></p><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c2">- Gross Amount = Original Course Price - Coupon Discount (if coupon is applied).</span></p><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c2">- Net Course Revenue to Split = Gross Amount - Stripe Processing Fee.</span></p><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c2">- Platform Net Profit = Net Course Revenue to Split * (100% - Transfer Rate %).</span></p><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c2">- Instructor Net Income = Net Course Revenue to Split * Transfer Rate %.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-194</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c11">Instructor Status Constraint :</span><span class="c2">Only active instructors whose registration application has been reviewed and approved by the platform administrator can access the course revenue dashboard.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-195</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c11">Period-Based Revenue Calculations:</span><span class="c33">Monthly, yearly, and lifetime revenues are dynamically calculated and loaded based on the active month and year filter selected.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-196</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c11">Month-over-Month Growth Calculation :</span><span class="c2">Growth percentage is calculated using the formula: Growth % = ((Current Month Revenue - Previous Month Revenue) /
