Previous Month Revenue) * 100.</span></p><p class="c6 c86"><span class="c2">- If the previous month's revenue is zero and the current month's revenue is greater than zero, it is labeled and displayed as "New Sales".</span></p><p class="c6 c23 c86"><span class="c10"></span></p><p class="c6 c23 c86"><span class="c10"></span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-197</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c10">Course Revenue Calculations :</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">- Gross Revenue = Total sales revenue generated from all course purchases on the platform.</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">- Total Refunded = Total deductions from refund requests.</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c33">- Net Earnings = Gross Revenue - Total Refunded.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-198</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c10"> Splits and Distributions Calculations:</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">- Paid to Instructors = Total funds paid out/transferred to instructors' Stripe accounts or banks.</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">- Escrow Funds = Funds currently locked in the 14-day escrow protection window.</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c33">- Platform Net Profit = Net platform share cut after splits (Net Course Revenue to Split * (100% - Instructor Share %)).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-199</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c10">Stripe Balances Calculations:</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">- Stripe Balance Available Now = Available balance in the platform's Stripe Connect balance.</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">- Stripe Balance In Transit = Pending or incoming Stripe funds.</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c33">- Total Stripe Assets = Stripe Balance Available Now + Stripe Balance In Transit.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-200</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Withdrawal Representation :</span><span class="c33">The withdrawal amount is displayed as a deduction (colored in red, e.g. $32.28 representing the debit from the Stripe balance).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-201</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Full Customer Refund:</span><span class="c2"> The customer is refunded 100% of the original purchase amount via the Stripe Refund API.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-202</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Amount Negation: </span><span class="c2">The transaction Amount and the instructor's PayoutAmount are updated to negative numbers in the database to maintain double-entry accounting integrity.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-203</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Access Revocation: </span><span class="c2">The student's EnrollmentStatus is changed to revoked, immediately disabling course access.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-204</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c10">Claw Back Instructor Earnings (Transfer Reversal): </span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">The system reverses the exact payout amount originally transferred to the instructor's Connected Account:</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">PayoutAmount = Round((GrossAmount - StripeFee) * (TransferRate / 100), 2)</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">where:</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">StripeFee = Min(Round(GrossAmount * 0.029 + 0.30, 2), GrossAmount)</span></p><p class="c6 c23 c176 c121"><span class="c10"></span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-205</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c10">Rejection Reason Required: </span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">Unlike approval, rejecting a refund request strictly requires a non-empty administrative explanation to provide clarity to the student.</span></p><p class="c6 c23 c176 c121"><span class="c10"></span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-206</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Status Restored to Succeeded: </span><span class="c33">The transaction state returns to succeed as if the refund request was never initiated.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-207</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">No Financial Changes: </span><span class="c33">No funds are transferred, reversed, or refunded via Stripe Connect. The platform commissions and instructor earnings remain intact.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-208</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Uninterrupted Learning Access: </span><span class="c33">The student's course enrollment status (EnrollmentStatus) remains active, ensuring they continue to have full access to learning materials.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-209</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Refund Actions Visibility: </span><span class="c2">The "Approve Refund" and "Reject" buttons are conditionally rendered on the details page only if the transaction status is exactly refund_pending.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-210</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c10">Refund Info Retrieval: </span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c33">If a refund request was ever made, the student's submitted refund reason and the request date are fetched from TransactionExt and displayed. If rejected, the Admin's rejection note is also shown.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-211</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c10">Permitted Splitting Range: </span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">The split rate must be a numeric value between 30.00% and 95.00% (inclusive).</span></p><p class="c6 c23 c176 c121"><span class="c10"></span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-212</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c10">Non-Retroactivity: </span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">Changing the configuration only applies to transactions created after the change. Existing payouts and past transaction records preserve their original split rate values.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-213</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c10">Instructor Policy Notifications:</span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c2">The system sends an automated real-time notification to all active instructors who have connected Stripe accounts. Re-notification is bypassed for non-Stripe linked accounts.</span></p><p class="c6 c23 c176 c121"><span class="c10"></span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-214</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Permitted Payout Days Range: </span><span class="c33">The trigger days must be integers between 15 and 20 (inclusive), formatted as a single number or a comma-separated list of numbers (e.g., 15 or 15, 18).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-215</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c10">Scheduler Automation: </span></p><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c33">The automated bulk payout scheduler queries this setting and automatically initiates payouts to instructors' Connect accounts on the configured days of the month.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c250"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-216</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Minimum Withdrawal Limit: </span><span class="c33">Platform withdrawals must be at least $0.50 (USD).</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-217</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Maximum Available Balance: </span><span class="c33">The maximum amount allowed to be withdrawn cannot exceed the platform's current Stripe Available balance. Cents are rounded to two decimal places.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-218</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Widget Home-only Visibility: </span><span class="c2">The floating chat widget is visible and accessible only on the Home page for logged-in Users.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-219</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Support Ticket Queueing:</span><span class="c2"> Support requests submitted via the mini-chat widget create a pending request queue entry and prevent further messages until a staff member accepts the request.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-220</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Support Request Assignment:</span><span class="c33"> Pending support tickets are visible only to Staff members. When a Staff member clicks `"Accept Request"`, the ticket is assigned to them and removed from the public pending list. Admin members do not receive or view pending support requests from users.</span><span class="c10"> </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-221</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Support Request Assignment:</span><span class="c33"> Pending support tickets are visible only to Staff members. When a Staff member clicks `"Accept Request"`, the ticket is assigned to them and removed from the public pending list. Admin members do not receive or view pending support requests from users.</span><span class="c10"> </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c342"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-222</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Gmail Exclusive: </span><span class="c2">Only emails ending with "@gmail.com" are permitted for registration.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c526"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-223</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Uniqueness: </span><span class="c2">Email and username checks are case-insensitive and must be unique across the platform.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c298"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-224</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Security: </span><span class="c2">Passwords must be hashed using BCrypt before database storage.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c225"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-225</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Default Account Status: </span><span class="c2">Registered accounts are initialized with status "Active", AuthProvider "Local", and verification flag set to false.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c486"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-226</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">OTP Life Cycle: </span><span class="c2">The OTP code must be consumed upon validation (one-time use only) and expires within 2 minutes of generation.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c738"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-227</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Session Cookie Sync: </span><span class="c2">The system must update the IsVerified cookie to "true" immediately on successful verification to dynamically unlock shopping and checkout permissions without requiring a re-login.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c648"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-228</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">External Account Prevention:</span><span class="c2"> Users who registered using third-party auth (Google OAuth) are prohibited from changing password as they do not have a password hash stored locally.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c322"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-229</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Forced Logout: </span><span class="c2">On a successful password change, the system must immediately revoke the refresh token, delete client-side cookies, and force the User to authenticate again to prevent active hijacked sessions.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c440"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-230</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Google Email Lock: </span><span class="c2">Email modification is strictly locked for Google OAuth accounts to prevent authorization mismatches.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-231</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Transactional Integrity: </span><span class="c2">Profile modifications spanning User and Account database tables must be executed within a single transaction. If any part of the query fails (e.g. duplicate email checks), the transaction rolls back completely.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-232</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Session Synchronization: </span><span class="c2">Client-side display cookies (such as UserName and AvatarUrl) must be immediately updated on success to ensure the page headers dynamically reflect the changes.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-233</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Verification Syncing: </span><span class="c2">During the profile retrieval process, the system must dynamically read the database verification flag and update the client-side IsVerified cookie to ensure consistent page behavior and menu permissions. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c503"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-234</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Uniqueness Constraint: </span><span class="c2">A specific course can be added to a User's wishlist only once.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c154"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-235</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">AJAX Toggle: </span><span class="c2">The add action is handled asynchronously via AJAX (ToggleAjax) so the UI changes state instantly without a page reload. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c322"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-236 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Default Statistics:</span><span class="c2"> Average rating and total students are aggregated dynamically. If statistics are missing for a course, a default rating of 4.5 is displayed. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c724"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-237 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Permanent Deletion: </span><span class="c2">Unlike coupons or profiles, wishlist items are physically hard-deleted from the database when removed, as they do not require transaction history preservation. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-238 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Unique Username Generation:</span><span class="c2"> For new Google sign-ups, the username is extracted from the email prefix. If the username is already taken, the system appends a numeric counter sequentially (e.g. johndoe1, johndoe2) until it is unique. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c145"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-239 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Auto-Verification: </span><span class="c2">Accounts registered via Google OAuth are flagged as verified (IsVerified = true) immediately upon creation because Google has verified the email ownership. Null Password Hash: Google OAuth accounts are initialized with no password hash (PasswordHash = null), bypassing local password authentication. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-240 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Suspension Block: </span><span class="c2">If an active lockout exists for the account, the system denies login and prints the suspension lockout end date in the error message. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c440"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-241 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Credential Flexibility: </span><span class="c2">The login identifier can be either the account's username or email address (case-insensitive). </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c694"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-242 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">BCrypt Verification: </span><span class="c2">Local password validation must be processed using BCrypt.Verify.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-243 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Role-Based Redirection: </span><span class="c2">Authenticated Admin and Staff actors are redirected to the admin dashboard, while Users and Instructors are redirected to the homepage. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-244 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Validity Criteria: </span><span class="c2">To be applied, a coupon must be active, within its validity dates (StartDate &lt;= Now &lt;= EndDate), have remaining uses (UsedCount &lt; UsageLimit), and the cart subtotal must meet the MinOrderValue. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-245 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Discount Logic:</span><span class="c2"> Percentage/Fixed discount calculation and Session Cookie Storage: Applied coupons are written to local cookies to maintain the discount status across browsing activities until checkout. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-246 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Cookie Invalidation: </span><span class="c2">The coupon code must be completely deleted from local session cookies. Price Reversion: Removing a coupon must immediately restore the cart's discount value to zero. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-247 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Verified Email Requirement: </span><span class="c2">Only accounts that have completed email verification are permitted to use the forget password reset feature. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-248 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Token Invalidation/Cookie Clearance: </span><span class="c2">Upon logout, the backend must immediately nullify/revoke the stored RefreshToken and clear all client-side cookies. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-249 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Required Credentials: </span><span class="c2">Email, Password, and Display Name are mandatory fields that cannot be empty. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-250 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Auto-Verification: </span><span class="c2">Since the account is directly created by the Admin, the new Staff account is initialized as verified (IsVerified = true) and active (AccountStatus = "Active") by default. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-251 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Role Scope Restriction: </span><span class="c33">This update endpoint is restricted solely to accounts carrying the "staff" role.</span><span class="c10"> </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-252 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Ban/Lockout Logic: </span><span class="c2">Banning adds a 100-year lockout record; Unbanning deletes all active lockout entries to restore login access. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-253 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Super Admin Protection:</span><span class="c2"> The Super Admin account ("admin" role) is protected and cannot be banned or locked out. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-254 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Role-Specific Data Aggregation: </span><span class="c2">System displays data based on specific roles (Students, Instructors, Managers/Staff) and detects lockouts if the account has been banned. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-255 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Search Criteria: </span><span class="c2">Search keywords are matched (case-insensitive substring) against the account's Email, Phone Number, Manager Display Name, or User Full Name. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-256 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Search Criteria: </span><span class="c2">Search keywords are matched (case-insensitive substring) against the account's Email, Phone Number, Manager Display Name, or User Full Name. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-257 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Default Pagination: </span><span class="c2">The page size defaults to 10 accounts per page if not specified otherwise. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-258 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Role Filters:</span><span class="c2"> Supported role filters include: "user", "instructor", "staff", "registered_staff", or "all" roles. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-259 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Flag Thresholds and Status Progression: </span><span class="c2">1st/2nd flag (warning); 3rd flag (banned) triggers a 30-day lockout. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-260 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Non-Blocking Notifications: </span><span class="c2">A notification dispatch error must not block or roll back the flagging transaction.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-261</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Unique: </span><span class="c2">Code, Coupon Types, Discount Value Limits, Validity Dates, and Usage Limit constraints for new coupons. </span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-262 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Sorting/Search: </span><span class="c2">Default sorting by ID, client-side in-memory sorting, and case-insensitive CouponCode matching.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-263</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Locked Fields: </span><span class="c2">Code, Type, Value, and MinOrderValue are read-only. End Date and Usage Limit have specific update constraints.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-264 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Soft-Delete Only: </span><span class="c2">Hard deletion is forbidden. Expiration Enforcement: End Date updated to the previous day to ensure the coupon becomes unusable.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-265 </span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c176 c121"><span class="c11">Filter Parameters: </span><span class="c2">Filtering by IsActive state, CouponType, or both combined in a single database query.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-266</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c33">Lockout Status Monitoring: The profile displays any active suspension records (start and end times) for the manager account, keeping administrative users informed of active lockout configurations.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-267</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c2">Role-based Labeling: The interface shows "Super Admin" if the manager's role is "ADMIN", and "Staff Support" otherwise.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-268</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Unique Quiz Title:</span><span class="c2"> The quiz title must be unique per instructor. The system checks this upon quiz creation and updates.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-269</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Minimum Total Questions:</span><span class="c2"> The total questions limit for a quiz must be greater than 0.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-270</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Distribution Integrity:</span><span class="c2"> The sum of question counts distributed across lessons must exactly equal the quiz's configured TotalQuestions.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-271</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Question Bank Sufficiency:</span><span class="c2"> For each lesson distribution, the number of requested questions must not exceed the actual number of questions available in the Question Bank for that specific lesson.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-272</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Attempt Lockdown:</span><span class="c2"> An instructor cannot update the settings of a quiz, delete a quiz, or remove a quiz from a course if there is currently an active (unsubmitted) attempt by any student.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-273</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Enrolled Course Protection:</span><span class="c2"> A quiz cannot be deleted if it is assigned to any course that has enrolled students.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-274</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Pending Course Lockdown:</span><span class="c2"> Quizzes cannot be added to, removed from, or toggled hidden in a course while the course status is "Pending".</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-275</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Auto Draft Fallback:</span><span class="c2"> Adding or removing a quiz from a "Published" course will automatically revert the course status back to "Draft".</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-276</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Quiz Generation Logic:</span><span class="c2"> When a student starts a quiz, the system randomly draws questions from the Question Bank based on the quiz's QuizLessonDistribution. If the distribution falls short, it supplements remaining questions randomly from the entire course's question pool.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-277</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Score Calculation:</span><span class="c2"> The attempt score is calculated as (Correct Answers / Total Questions) * 100, rounded to the nearest integer.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-278</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Passing Condition:</span><span class="c2"> A student attempt is marked as "Passed" if the calculated score is greater than or equal to the quiz's PassingScore configuration.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-279</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Minimum Options:</span><span class="c2"> A question must have at least 2 options.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-280</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Minimum Correct Options:</span><span class="c2"> A question must have at least 1 correct option.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-281</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6 c121"><span class="c11">Instructor Course Ownership:</span><span class="c2"> An instructor can only add, edit, delete, or view questions for courses they own.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-282</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c12"><span class="c11"> Instructor Payout Calculation</span><span class="c11">: </span><span class="c33">Stripe payment gateway fees and the system commission rate are deducted from the actual selling price to calculate the instructor's payout amount.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-283</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c12"><span class="c11"> Payout Holding Period:</span><span class="c33">The instructor's payout is held in a "Pending" status for 14 days to accommodate student refund policies or dispute claims.</span></p></td></tr><tr class="c198"><td class="c35" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c6"><span class="c2">BR-284</span></p></td><td class="c39" colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p class="c12"><span class="c11">Dynamic Currency Selection: </span><span class="c33">The payment currency is dynamically set based on the country of the instructor's Stripe account (e.g., USD,).</span></p></td></tr></table>
